This spec owns the items that change existing code.\n\n## Goal & Context\n\nThe model workspace (~86k lines of Rust) hand-rolls a few things that admitted or widely used crates do, and repeats several small helpers across crates. This spec replaces those with crates or one shared copy, moves hand-rolled golden comparisons onto insta, and drops the binary-snapshot layout that forced the fixture runner to decode insta's private file naming. Checker semantics, Model meaning and the semantic protocol contract are preserved; internal and wire representations may change with coordinated regeneration. Prost-build's default WKT mappings replace in-house well-known-type generation consistently across runtime, Kettle and shared Temporal codegen: nonprimitive types use `prost-types`, Empty uses unit and scalar/string/bytes wrappers use the corresponding Rust primitives; descriptor-derived paths use the same mapping. A thin wrapper keeps the Malformed-versus-Unsupported refusal split of `any.rs`. Recursion-only matches use them: `Expr::infallible` (only its non-recursive `false` arms stay explicit), `umpire-tla` `names::rename` and `emit::prime`, the fixtures' interpreted `rewrite`. raw identifier if the identifier matches a Rust keyword:\n    // https://doc.rust-lang.org/reference/keywords.html.\n    match ident {\n        // 2015 strict keywords.\n        | \"as\" | \"break\" | Catalog order is the `Action` enums' declaration order; `#[machine]` takes no arguments and emits `#[forbid(unsafe_code)]` on the module. Every attribute is pass-through (R36); nothing is rewritten. Errors: trybuild `.stderr` fixtures pin the out-of-order, `&mut self`-outside-transitions and leftover-group-attribute messages. match guards are the guards; `_ => {}` is \"disabled\" under .18's enabled-iff-changed rule; rustc's exhaustiveness replaces the missing-handler error when no `_` arm is written), optional `#[effect]` helpers (the only other methods allowed `&mut self`), `#[invariant]`, `#[postcondition]` and `#[postcondition(after = Heater::Failover)]`, `#[assumption]`, `#[progress]`. `#[machine]` enforces that items of one kind are contiguous and the kinds appear in the order on, effect, invariant, postcondition, assumption, progress, with a hard error at the first out-of-place item naming the kind it must follow and precede; unattributed helpers are ignored by the order check but refused if they take `&mut self` (`<method> takes &mut self; only #[on] and #[effect] methods change the state`); `#[machine]` emits `#[forbid(unsafe_code)]` on the module and takes no arguments (catalog order = the `Action` enums' declaration order). Catalog order is the `Action` enums' declaration order; `#[machine]` takes no arguments and emits `#[forbid(unsafe_code)]` on the module. Every attribute is pass-through (R36); nothing is rewritten.
